So, what does production planning mean? It means showing the way and how to use limited resources for a specific purpose. Planning allows economic use of constraints, as it increases efficiency. With the help of planning, the farmer can decide what products, how and how much to produce in agricultural production. Since there was no production planning by the Department of Agriculture, our farmer is trying to plant products at his own request, and he's trying to produce a product in his own way by planting whatever product cost was high that year. This causes more production of some products, while in some products it leads to inadequacies. Information in Turkey is easy to access, compiling and compiling information. In the inner Anatolian region, farmers are importing chickpeas, saying prices are attractive, starting to plant too many chickpeas. The farmer doesn't always get what he wants. Thus, according to the accumulation and internal properties of each region, the best product will begin to be produced according to the country's demand. The imports from the outside will be planned inside. Other areas demanding agricultural products will be made to produce according to consumer preferences. Again, the amendment to Article 62 of the proposal has been determined by Article 5607 to add a sentence for trafficking in Article 3 of the Anti-Trafficking Law, while the punishment for trafficking will be increased to onefold if the value of the object is exorbitant, while the charge is determined to be reduced to a reduction in the penalty, if the value of the illegal object is light or light. This amendment clearly states that the penalty will be reduced to 1/3, if the value of the object of the crime is low, and since the absence of the law causes various victims, it will be added as a clause of the law to prevent the victims. A amendment with Article 63 of the Proposal was estimated to be a partial reduction in the sentence until the end of the investigation period, if two times its customs value is paid to the state treasury by effective regret to the accused who committed trafficking crimes, which is a flaw in the second joke of the Law on Trafficking 5 ▪. While this clause was being made, some inequalities were also considered, especially in this substance, given the victimship of many people in the southeast and east, and in the event of the capture of small quantities of contraband, cigarettes, or alcohol. In this sense, it is positive that this is added into matter. In other words, there is no question of asking the republic's prosecutor to pay for its effective regret through notice. With this amendment, the prosecutor of the Republic has been obliged to warn the defendant whether he will comply with the active regret during the investigation phase, and if this has not been issued, it can be carried out by the judge during the prosecution. I can't help but point out that the notice made by the judge is one / three, while in the investigation phase, a reduction of 1/2 is mentioned. We believe that these reduction rates should be applied equally according to the principle of equality.
